                    orders.ord_num,
                    orders.PO_NUM,
                    ntfile.NOTES
                FROM 
                    DBO.ORD_LOG AS ord
                JOIN 
                    dbo.orders AS orders ON ord.ord_num = orders.ord_num
                JOIN
                    dbo.ntfile AS ntfile ON orders.ord_num = ntfile.nt_num

            )

                ord_num,
                PO_NUM,
                NOTES
            FROM 
                ExtractedNumbers
            ORDER BY 
                CASE 
                    WHEN ISNUMERIC(
                        LEFT(
                            UT_DESC,
                            CASE
                                WHEN CHARINDEX('-', UT_DESC) > 0 THEN CHARINDEX('-', UT_DESC) - 1
                                WHEN CHARINDEX(',', UT_DESC) > 0 THEN CHARINDEX(',', UT_DESC) - 1
                                ELSE LEN(UT_DESC)
                            END
                        )

                        LEFT(

                                WHEN CHARINDEX('-', UT_DESC) > 0 THEN CHARINDEX('-', UT_DESC) - 1

2024-10-03 13:57:46,374 - INFO - yeti executed a custom SQL query: WITH ExtractedNumbers AS (
            SELECT DISTINCT
                ord.UT_DESC,
                ord.PROD_CD,
                ord.PC_UNIT,
                ord.order_qty ,          -- Added ord_qty to the CTE
                orders.SP_ADR,
                orders.SP_ADR_2,
                orders.SP_ADR_22,
                orders.SP_ADR_3,
                orders.ord_num,
                orders.PO_NUM,
                ntfile.NOTES
            FROM 
                DBO.ORD_LOG AS ord
            JOIN 
                dbo.orders AS orders ON ord.ord_num = orders.ord_num
            JOIN
                dbo.ntfile AS ntfile ON orders.ord_num = ntfile.nt_num
            WHERE  
                ord.ord_num = 33345
        )
        SELECT 
            UT_DESC, 
            PROD_CD, 
            PC_UNIT, 
            SP_ADR, 
            SP_ADR_2, 
            SP_ADR_22, 
            SP_ADR_3,
            ord_num,
            PO_NUM,
            NOTES,
            order_qty % PC_UNIT AS Remainder  -- Calculating the remainder
        FROM 
            ExtractedNumbers
        ORDER BY 
            CASE 
                WHEN ISNUMERIC(
                    LEFT(
                        UT_DESC,
                        CASE
                            WHEN CHARINDEX('-', UT_DESC) > 0 THEN CHARINDEX('-', UT_DESC) - 1
                            WHEN CHARINDEX(',', UT_DESC) > 0 THEN CHARINDEX(',', UT_DESC) - 1
                            ELSE LEN(UT_DESC)
                        END
                    )
                ) = 1 THEN CAST(
                    LEFT(
                        UT_DESC,
                        CASE
                            WHEN CHARINDEX('-', UT_DESC) > 0 THEN CHARINDEX('-', UT_DESC) - 1
                            WHEN CHARINDEX(',', UT_DESC) > 0 THEN CHARINDEX(',', UT_DESC) - 1
                            ELSE LEN(UT_DESC)
                        END
                    ) AS INT)
                ELSE 1
            END;

                    orders.SP_ADR_22,
                    orders.SP_ADR_3,
                    orders.ord_num,
                    orders.PO_NUM,
                    ntfile.NOTES
                FROM 
                    DBO.ORD_LOG AS ord
                JOIN 
                    dbo.orders AS orders ON ord.ord_num = orders.ord_num
                JOIN
                    dbo.ntfile AS ntfile ON orders.ord_num = ntfile.nt_num
                WHERE  
                    ord.ord_num = 33339
            )
            SELECT 
                UT_DESC, 
                PROD_CD, 
                PC_UNIT, 
                SP_ADR, 
                SP_ADR_2, 
                SP_ADR_22, 
                SP_ADR_3,
                ord_num,
                PO_NUM,
                NOTES,
                order_qty,
                order_qty % PC_UNIT AS Remainder
            FROM 
                ExtractedNumbers
            ORDER BY 
                CASE 
                    WHEN ISNUMERIC(
                        LEFT(
                            UT_DESC,
                            CASE
                                WHEN CHARINDEX('-', UT_DESC) > 0 THEN CHARINDEX('-', UT_DESC) - 1
                                WHEN CHARINDEX(',', UT_DESC) > 0 THEN CHARINDEX(',', UT_DESC) - 1
                                ELSE LEN(UT_DESC)
                            END
                        )
                    ) = 1 THEN CAST(
                        LEFT(
                            UT_DESC,
                            CASE
                                WHEN CHARINDEX('-', UT_DESC) > 0 THEN CHARINDEX('-', UT_DESC) - 1
                                WHEN CHARINDEX(',', UT_DESC) > 0 THEN CHARINDEX(',', UT_DESC) - 1
                                ELSE LEN(UT_DESC)
                            END
                        ) AS INT)
                    ELSE 1
                END;
